{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMbhAN9EeQdEtCs3Nytd","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Ug1wTkAuD3m93fX1g6AQ7X","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T213208Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/contracts/src/TipJar.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.24;\n\nimport {IERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport {SafeERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\n\n/// @title TipJar\n/// @notice A USDC tip jar. Anyone can send a USDC tip with a short message.\n///         Tips accumulate in the contract; the owner can withdraw them.\n/// @dev USDC uses 6 decimals. Tippers must `approve` this contract to spend\n///      their USDC before calling `tip`.\ncontract TipJar {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    struct Tip {\n        address from; // who sent the tip\n        uint256 amount; // amount in USDC's smallest unit (6 decimals)\n        string message; // short public message\n        uint256 timestamp; // block time the tip was recorded\n    }\n\n    /// @notice The USDC token this jar accepts.\n    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n\n    /// @notice The address allowed to withdraw collected tips.\n    address public owner;\n\n    /// @notice Maximum length of a tip message, in bytes.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH = 280;\n\n    /// @notice All tips ever recorded, oldest first.\n    Tip[] private _tips;\n\n    /// @notice Running total of USDC tipped (across all withdrawals).\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n\n    event NewTip(address indexed from, uint256 amount, string message, uint256 timestamp);\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n    event OwnershipTransferred(address indexed previousOwner, address indexed newOwner);\n\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error MessageTooLong();\n    error NotOwner();\n    error NothingToWithdraw();\n    error ZeroAddress();\n\n    modifier onlyOwner() {\n        if (msg.sender != owner) revert NotOwner();\n        _;\n    }\n\n    /// @param _usdc Address of the USDC token contract.\n    /// @param _owner Address that may withdraw collected tips.\n    constructor(address _usdc, address _owner) {\n        if (_usdc == address(0) || _owner == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        usdc = IERC20(_usdc);\n        owner = _owner;\n        emit OwnershipTransferred(address(0), _owner);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Send a USDC tip with a message.\n    /// @dev Caller must have approved this contract for at least `amount` USDC.\n    /// @param amount Amount of USDC (6 decimals) to tip.\n    /// @param message A short public message (<= MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH bytes).\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (bytes(message).length > MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH) revert MessageTooLong();\n\n        // Pull the USDC from the tipper into this contract.\n        usdc.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n\n        _tips.push(Tip({from: msg.sender, amount: amount, message: message, timestamp: block.timestamp}));\n        totalTipped += amount;\n\n        emit NewTip(msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Number of tips recorded.\n    function tipCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _tips.length;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Read a single tip by index.\n    function getTip(uint256 index) external view returns (Tip memory) {\n        return _tips[index];\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Read all tips (oldest first). Convenient for a local UI.\n    function getTips() external view returns (Tip[] memory) {\n        return _tips;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Read the most recent `count` tips, newest first.\n    /// @param count Maximum number of tips to return.\n    function getRecentTips(uint256 count) external view returns (Tip[] memory) {\n        uint256 len = _tips.length;\n        if (count > len) count = len;\n        Tip[] memory out = new Tip[](count);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n            out[i] = _tips[len - 1 - i];\n        }\n        return out;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Current USDC balance held by the jar (tips not yet withdrawn).\n    function balance() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw the entire USDC balance to the owner.\n    function withdraw() external onlyOwner {\n        uint256 bal = usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (bal == 0) revert NothingToWithdraw();\n        usdc.safeTransfer(owner, bal);\n        emit Withdrawn(owner, bal);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Transfer ownership (withdrawal rights) to a new address.\n    function transferOwnership(address newOwner) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newOwner == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        address prev = owner;\n        owner = newOwner;\n        emit OwnershipTransferred(prev, newOwner);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":404,"cache_read_input_tokens":23071,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":404},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"20b4b874-e367-47cf-b746-716131007b23","uuid":"bae84595-c49a-4b30-95a2-b97f61c540db","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:33:45.101Z","request_id":"req_011CdMbh9PrhwimfsKqYZvNG"}

Anyone can send a USDC tip with a short message.\n///         Tips accumulate in the contract; the owner can withdraw them.\n/// @dev USDC uses 6 decimals. Tippers must `approve` this contract to spend\n///      their USDC before calling `tip`.\ncontract TipJar {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    struct Tip {\n        address from; // who sent the tip\n        uint256 amount; // amount in USDC's smallest unit (6 decimals)\n        string message; // short public message\n        uint256 timestamp; // block time the tip was recorded\n    }\n\n    /// @notice The USDC token this jar accepts.\n    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n\n    /// @notice The address allowed to withdraw collected tips.\n    address public owner;\n\n    /// @notice Maximum length of a tip message, in bytes.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH = 280;\n\n    /// @notice All tips ever recorded, oldest first.\n    Tip[] private _tips;\n\n    /// @notice Running total of USDC tipped (across all withdrawals).\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n\n    event NewTip(address indexed from, uint256 amount, string message, uint256 timestamp);\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n    event OwnershipTransferred(address indexed previousOwner, address indexed newOwner);\n\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error MessageTooLong();\n    error NotOwner();\n    error NothingToWithdraw();\n    error ZeroAddress();\n\n    modifier onlyOwner() {\n        if (msg.sender != owner) revert NotOwner();\n        _;\n    }\n\n    /// @param _usdc Address of the USDC token contract.\n    /// @param _owner Address that may withdraw collected tips.\n    constructor(address _usdc, address _owner) {\n        if (_usdc == address(0) || _owner == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        usdc = IERC20(_usdc);\n        owner = _owner;\n        emit OwnershipTransferred(address(0), _owner);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Send a USDC tip with a message.\n    /// @dev Caller must have approved this contract for at least `amount` USDC.\n    /// @param amount Amount of USDC (6 decimals) to tip.\n    /// @param message A short public message (<= MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH bytes).\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (bytes(message).length > MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH) revert MessageTooLong();\n\n        // Pull the USDC from the tipper into this contract.\n        usdc.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n\n        _tips.push(Tip({from: msg.sender, amount: amount, message: message, timestamp: block.timestamp}));\n        totalTipped += amount;\n\n        emit NewTip(msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Number of tips recorded.\n    function tipCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _tips.length;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Read a single tip by index.\n    function getTip(uint256 index) external view returns (Tip memory) {\n        return _tips[index];\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Read all tips (oldest first). Convenient for a local UI.\n    function getTips() external view returns (Tip[] memory) {\n        return _tips;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Read the most recent `count` tips, newest first.\n    /// @param count Maximum number of tips to return.\n    function getRecentTips(uint256 count) external view returns (Tip[] memory) {\n        uint256 len = _tips.length;\n        if (count > len) count = len;\n        Tip[] memory out = new Tip[](count);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n            out[i] = _tips[len - 1 - i];\n        }\n        return out;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Current USDC balance held by the jar (tips not yet withdrawn).\n    function balance() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw the entire USDC balance to the owner.\n    function withdraw() external onlyOwner {\n        uint256 bal = usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (bal == 0) revert NothingToWithdraw();\n        usdc.safeTransfer(owner, bal);\n        emit Withdrawn(owner, bal);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Transfer ownership (withdrawal rights) to a new address.\n    function transferOwnership(address newOwner) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newOwner == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        address prev = owner;\n        owner = newOwner;\n        emit OwnershipTransferred(prev, newOwner);\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

Never deploy this to a real network.\ncontract MockUSDC is ERC20 {\n    uint8 private constant _DECIMALS = 6;\n\n    constructor() ERC20(\"Mock USD Coin\", \"USDC\") {}\n\n    function decimals() public pure override returns (uint8) {\n        return _DECIMALS;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Mint `amount` (6 decimals) of test USDC to `to`.\n    function mint(address to, uint256 amount) external {\n        _mint(to, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Mint 1,000 test USDC to the caller.\n    function faucet() external {\n        _mint(msg.sender, 1_000 * 10 ** _DECIMALS);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2565,"cache_read_input_tokens":23475,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2565},"output_tokens":51,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"20b4b874-e367-47cf-b746-716131007b23","uuid":"723b5253-9a7d-47ff-9036-c5357c914d95","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:33:52.544Z","request_id":"req_011CdMbj2zwMvHi6rnGcq241"}

Never deploy this to a real network.\ncontract MockUSDC is ERC20 {\n    uint8 private constant _DECIMALS = 6;\n\n    constructor() ERC20(\"Mock USD Coin\", \"USDC\") {}\n\n    function decimals() public pure override returns (uint8) {\n        return _DECIMALS;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Mint `amount` (6 decimals) of test USDC to `to`.\n    function mint(address to, uint256 amount) external {\n        _mint(to, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Mint 1,000 test USDC to the caller.\n    function faucet() external {\n        _mint(msg.sender, 1_000 * 10 ** _DECIMALS);\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

It will deploy MockUSDC + TipJar and write addresses to a JSON the frontend reads."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":645,"cache_read_input_tokens":26040,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":645},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"20b4b874-e367-47cf-b746-716131007b23","uuid":"3f214306-6efa-4730-b90f-c87ac60a1897","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:34:06.411Z","request_id":"req_011CdMbjajuVjRKtJLqicfwM"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMbjbhwup77PJPHK2m5U","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01TTiiCr3sHo4uroHYfAurCe","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T213208Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/contracts/script/Deploy.s.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.24;\n\nimport {Script, console} from \"forge-std/Script.sol\";\nimport {TipJar} from \"../src/TipJar.sol\";\nimport {MockUSDC} from \"../src/MockUSDC.sol\";\n\n/// @notice Deploys a MockUSDC + TipJar to a LOCAL chain (e.g. anvil), seeds the\n///         deployer with test USDC, and writes the deployed addresses to\n///         ../frontend/contracts/deployment.json so the web app can find them.\n///\n/// Usage:\n///   forge script script/Deploy.s.sol:Deploy \\\n///     --rpc-url http://127.0.0.1:8545 --broadcast\ncontract Deploy is Script {\n    // On a real deployment you would point TipJar at Base USDC:\n    //   0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\n    // For local dev we deploy a mock instead.\n    function run() external {\n        uint256 deployerKey = vm.envOr(\n            \"PRIVATE_KEY\",\n            // Default: anvil account #0 (well-known local test key).\n            uint256(0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80)\n        );\n        address deployer = vm.addr(deployerKey);\n\n        vm.startBroadcast(deployerKey);\n\n        MockUSDC usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        TipJar tipJar = new TipJar(address(usdc), deployer);\n\n        // Seed the deployer with 10,000 test USDC so tips can be sent immediately.\n        usdc.mint(deployer, 10_000 * 10 ** 6);\n\n        vm.stopBroadcast();\n\n        console.log(\"MockUSDC deployed at:\", address(usdc));\n        console.log(\"TipJar  deployed at:\", address(tipJar));\n        console.log(\"Owner / deployer     :\", deployer);\n\n        _writeFrontendConfig(address(usdc), address(tipJar));\n    }\n\n    function _writeFrontendConfig(address usdc, address tipJar) internal {\n        string memory json = string.concat(\n            \"{\\n\",\n            '  \"chainId\": ',\n            vm.toString(block.chainid),\n            \",\\n\",\n            '  \"MockUSDC\": need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"20b4b874-e367-47cf-b746-716131007b23","uuid":"94e0884c-414c-4601-8b58-59d9359edf45","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:34:20.428Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T213208Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/contracts/script/Deploy.s.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.24;\n\nimport {Script, console} from \"forge-std/Script.sol\";\nimport {TipJar} from \"../src/TipJar.sol\";\nimport {MockUSDC} from \"../src/MockUSDC.sol\";\n\n/// @notice Deploys a MockUSDC + TipJar to a LOCAL chain (e.g. anvil), seeds the\n///         deployer with test USDC, and writes the deployed addresses to\n///         ../frontend/contracts/deployment.json so the web app can find them.\n///\n/// Usage:\n///   forge script script/Deploy.s.sol:Deploy \\\n///     --rpc-url http://127.0.0.1:8545 --broadcast\ncontract Deploy is Script {\n    // On a real deployment you would point TipJar at Base USDC:\n    //   0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\n    // For local dev we deploy a mock instead.\n    function run() external {\n        uint256 deployerKey = vm.envOr(\n            \"PRIVATE_KEY\",\n            // Default: anvil account #0 (well-known local test key).\n            uint256(0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80)\n        );\n        address deployer = vm.addr(deployerKey);\n\n        vm.startBroadcast(deployerKey);\n\n        MockUSDC usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        TipJar tipJar = new TipJar(address(usdc), deployer);\n\n        // Seed the deployer with 10,000 test USDC so tips can be sent immediately.\n        usdc.mint(deployer, 10_000 * 10 ** 6);\n\n        vm.stopBroadcast();\n\n        console.log(\"MockUSDC deployed at:\", address(usdc));\n        console.log(\"TipJar  deployed at:\", address(tipJar));\n        console.log(\"Owner / deployer     :\", deployer);\n\n        _writeFrontendConfig(address(usdc), address(tipJar));\n    }\n\n    function _writeFrontendConfig(address usdc, address tipJar) internal {\n        string memory json = string.concat(\n            \"{\\n\",\n            '  \"chainId\": ',\n            vm.toString(block.chainid),\n            \",\\n\",\n            '  \"MockUSDC\": \"',\n            vm.toString(usdc),\n            '\",\\n',\n            '  \"TipJar\": \"',\n            vm.toString(tipJar),\n            '\"\\n',\n            \"}\\n\"\n        );\n        vm.writeFile(\"../frontend/contracts/deployment.json\", json);\n        console.log(\"Wrote ../frontend/contracts/deployment.json\");\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMbn2J73yQqVmd8xrP8s","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Qj5paESJx447SXFaRsdLVt","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T213208Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/contracts/test/TipJar.t.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.24;\n\nimport {Test} from \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\nimport {TipJar} from \"../src/TipJar.sol\";\nimport {MockUSDC} from \"../src/MockUSDC.sol\";\n\ncontract TipJarTest is Test {\n    MockUSDC internal usdc;\n    TipJar internal jar;\n\n    address internal owner = address(0xABCD);\n    address internal alice = address(0xA11CE);\n    address internal bob = address(0xB0B);\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        jar = new TipJar(address(usdc), owner);\n\n        usdc.mint(alice, 1_000e6);\n        usdc.mint(bob, 1_000e6);\n    }\n\n    function _tip(address who, uint256 amount, string memory msg_) internal {\n        vm.startPrank(who);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), amount);\n        jar.tip(amount, msg_);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_TipRecordsFeedAndTransfersUsdc() public {\n        _tip(alice, 10e6, \"gm\");\n\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 1);\n        assertEq(jar.balance(), 10e6);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 10e6);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 990e6);\n\n        TipJar.Tip memory t = jar.getTip(0);\n        assertEq(t.from, alice);\n        assertEq(t.amount, 10e6);\n        assertEq(t.message, \"gm\");\n        assertEq(t.timestamp, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    function test_GetRecentTipsNewestFirst() public {\n        _tip(alice, 1e6, \"first\");\n        _tip(bob, 2e6, \"second\");\n        _tip(alice, 3e6, \"third\");\n\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory recent = jar.getRecentTips(2);\n        assertEq(recent.length, 2);\n        assertEq(recent[0].message, \"third\");\n        assertEq(recent[1].message, \"second\");\n\n        // Requesting more than exist returns all of them.\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory all = jar.getRecentTips(50);\n        assertEq(all.length, 3);\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertOnZeroAmount() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), 1e6);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAmount.selector);\n        jar.tip(0, \"nope\");\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertOnMessageTooLong() public {\n        string memory long = new string(281);\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), 1e6);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.MessageTooLong.selector);\n        jar.tip(1e6, long);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertWhenNotApproved() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(); // SafeERC20 / allowance failure\n        jar.tip(1e6, \"no approval\");\n    }\n\n    function test_OwnerCanWithdraw() public {\n        _tip(alice, 10e6, \"gm\");\n        _tip(bob, 5e6, \"ty\");\n\n        uint256 before = usdc.balanceOf(owner);\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        jar.withdraw();\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), before + 15e6);\n        assertEq(jar.balance(), 0);\n        // Feed history is preserved after withdrawal.\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 2);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 15e6);\n    }\n\n    function test_NonOwnerCannotWithdraw() public {\n        _tip(alice, 10e6, \"gm\");\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NotOwner.selector);\n        jar.withdraw();\n    }\n\n    function test_WithdrawRevertsWhenEmpty() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NothingToWithdraw.selector);\n        jar.withdraw();\n    }\n\n    function test_TransferOwnership() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        jar.transferOwnership(alice);\n        assertEq(jar.owner(), alice);\n    }\n\n    function testFuzz_Tip(uint256 amount) public {\n        amount = bound(amount, 1, 1_000e6);\n        _tip(alice, amount, \"fuzz\");\n        assertEq(jar.balance(), amount);\n        assertEq(jar.getTip(0).amount, amount);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":470,"cache_read_input_tokens":28743,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":470},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"20b4b874-e367-47cf-b746-716131007b23","uuid":"f1b410d0-6dcf-40c2-828f-718e44306c2f","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:34:43.674Z","request_id":"req_011CdMbmyn3erVYhfrvoUwpW"}

memory recent = jar.getRecentTips(2);\n        assertEq(recent.length, 2);\n        assertEq(recent[0].message, \"third\");\n        assertEq(recent[1].message, \"second\");\n\n        // Requesting more than exist returns all of them.\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory all = jar.getRecentTips(50);\n        assertEq(all.length, 3);\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertOnZeroAmount() public {\n vm.expectRevert(TipJar.MessageTooLong.selector);\n        jar.tip(1e6, long);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertWhenNotApproved() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(); // SafeERC20 / allowance failure\n        jar.tip(1e6, \"no approval\");\n    }\n\n    function test_OwnerCanWithdraw() public {\n        _tip(alice, 10e6, \"gm\");\n        _tip(bob, 5e6, \"ty\");\n\n        uint256 before = usdc.balanceOf(owner);\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        jar.withdraw();\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), before + 15e6);\n        assertEq(jar.balance(), 0);\n        // Feed history is preserved after withdrawal.\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 2);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 15e6);\n    }\n\n    function {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMbvpTU8TBGiKJBT8T3a","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Un7W97PapNVaAe326u8CXs","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T213208Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/frontend/lib/wagmi.ts","content":"import { getDefaultConfig } from \"@rainbow-me/rainbowkit\";\nimport { defineChain } from \"viem\";\nimport { CHAIN_ID } from \"../contracts\";\n\nconst RPC_URL =\n  process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_RPC_URL ?? \"http://127.0.0.1:8545\";\n\n// A local Anvil / Hardhat chain. Matches the chain the contracts are deployed\n// to (see contracts/script/Deploy.s.sol). Chain id 31337 by default.\nexport const localChain = defineChain({\n  id: CHAIN_ID,\n  name: \"Anvil (local)\",\n  nativeCurrency: { name: \"Ether\", symbol: \"ETH\", decimals: 18 },\n  rpcUrls: {\n    default: { http: [RPC_URL] },\n  },\n  testnet: true,\n});\n\n// RainbowKit needs a WalletConnect projectId. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMbwUWSZYh67YJcCUig1","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01M5hMCPzme3z8T1eaqT9T8r","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T213208Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/frontend/lib/format.ts","content":"import { formatUnits } from \"viem\";\n\nexport const USDC_DECIMALS = 6;\n\n/** Format a USDC amount (6-decimal base units) as a human string like \"12.50\". */\nexport function formatUsdc(amount: bigint): string {\n  const asNumber = Number(formatUnits(amount, USDC_DECIMALS));\n  return asNumber.toLocaleString(undefined, {\n    minimumFractionDigits: 2,\n    maximumFractionDigits: 2,\n  });\n}\n\n/** Shorten an address like 0x1234…abcd. */\nexport function shortenAddress(address: string): string {\n  return `${address.slice(0, 6)}…${address.slice(-4)}`;\n}\n\n/** Relative time from a unix-seconds timestamp. */\nexport function timeAgo(unixSeconds: bigint): string {\n  const seconds = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) - Number(unixSeconds);\n  if (seconds < 60) return \"just now\";\n  const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);\n  if (minutes < 60) return `${minutes}m ago`;\n  const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);\n  if (hours < 24) return `${hours}h ago`;\n  const days = Math.floor(hours / 24);\n  return `${days}d ago`;\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":809,"cache_read_input_tokens":40695,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":809},"output_tokens":51,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"20b4b874-e367-47cf-b746-716131007b23","uuid":"1deae5fb-700d-42fd-b487-9f3f76284d19","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:36:40.641Z","request_id":"req_011CdMbwTRTKrDcm7bVMq783"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMbxkzuj28VsQBaQHUki","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01VGWTBQNYWD2t5m1di6fWA6","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T213208Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/frontend/components/TipForm.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport {\n  useAccount,\n  usePublicClient,\n  useReadContract,\n  useWriteContract,\n} from \"wagmi\";\nimport {\n  TIP_JAR_ADDRESS,\n  USDC_ADDRESS,\n  tipJarAbi,\n  usdcAbi,\n} from \"../contracts\";\nimport { formatUsdc } from \"../lib/format\";\n\nconst MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH = 280;\n\nexport function TipForm({ onTipped }: { onTipped: () => void }) {\n  const { address, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const publicClient = usePublicClient();\n  const { writeContractAsync } = useWriteContract();\n\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"5\");\n  const [message, setMessage] = useState(\"\");\n  const [status, setStatus] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);\n\n  const { data: balance, refetch: refetchBalance } = useReadContract({\n    address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n    abi: usdcAbi,\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: address ? [address] : undefined,\n    query: { enabled: !!address, refetchInterval: 5000 },\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance, refetch: refetchAllowance } = useReadContract({\n    address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n    abi: usdcAbi,\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: address ? [address, TIP_JAR_ADDRESS] : undefined,\n    query: { enabled: !!address },\n  });\n\n  async function handleFaucet() {\n    setError(null);\n    setStatus(null);\n    try {\n      setBusy(true);\n      setStatus(\"Requesting test USDC from the faucet…\");\n      const hash = await writeContractAsync({\n        address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n        abi: usdcAbi,\n        functionName: \"faucet\",\n      });\n      await publicClient?.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash });\n      await refetchBalance();\n      setStatus(\"Received 1,000 test USDC 🎉\");\n    } catch (err) {\n      setError(readableError(err));\n      setStatus(null);\n    } finally {\n      setBusy(false);\n    }\n  }\n\n  async function handleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    setError(null);\n    setStatus(null);\n\n    let amountBase: bigint;\n    try {\n      amountBase = parseUnits(amount || \"0\", 6);\n    } catch {\n      setError(\"Enter a valid USDC amount.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (amountBase <= 0n) {\n      setError(\"Tip amount must be greater than zero.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (new TextEncoder().encode(message).length > MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH) {\n      setError(`Message must be ${MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH} bytes or fewer.`);\n      return;\n    }\n    if (balance !== undefined && amountBase > (balance as bigint)) {\n      setError(\"You don't have enough USDC. Try the faucet below.\");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    try {\n      setBusy(true);\n\n      // Step 1: approve the tip jar to pull USDC, if the allowance is too low.\n      if ((allowance as bigint | undefined ?? 0n) < amountBase) {\n        setStatus(\"Approving USDC… confirm in your wallet.\");\n        const approveHash = await writeContractAsync({\n          address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n          abi: usdcAbi,\n          functionName: \"approve\",\n          args: [TIP_JAR_ADDRESS, amountBase],\n        });\n        await publicClient?.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: approveHash });\n        await refetchAllowance();\n      }\n\n      // Step 2: send the tip.\n      setStatus(\"Sending your tip… confirm in your wallet.\");\n      const tipHash = await writeContractAsync({\n        address: TIP_JAR_ADDRESS,\n        abi: tipJarAbi,\n        functionName: \"tip\",\n        args: [amountBase, message],\n      });\n      await publicClient?.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: tipHash });\n\n      setStatus(\"Tip sent! Thank you 💙\");\n      setMessage(\"\");\n      await Promise.all([refetchBalance(), refetchAllowance()]);\n      onTipped();\n    } catch (err) {\n      setError(readableError(err));\n      setStatus(null);\n    } finally {\n      setBusy(false);\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (!isConnected) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"card\">\n        <h2>Send a tip</h2>\n        <p className=\"muted\">Connect your wallet to send a USDC tip.</p>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card\">\n      <h2>Send a tip</h2>\n      <p className=\"muted\">\n        Your balance:{\" \"}\n        <strong>\n          {balance !== undefined ? `${formatUsdc(balance as bigint)} USDC` : \"…\"}\n        </strong>\n      </p>\n\n      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className=\"tip-form\">\n        <label>\n          Amount (USDC)\n          <input\n            type=\"number\"\n            min=\"0\"\n            step=\"0.01\"\n            value={amount}\n            onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            disabled={busy}\n            required\n          />\n        </label>\n\n        <label>\n          Message\n          <textarea\n            value={message}\n            onChange={(e) => setMessage(e.target.value)}\n            placeholder=\"Say something nice (optional)\"\n            maxLength={MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH}\n            rows={2}\n            disabled={busy}\n          />\n          <span className=\"counter\">\n            {message.length}/{MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH}\n          </span>\n        </label>\n\n        <div className=\"actions\">\n          <button type=\"submit\" className=\"primary\" disabled={busy}>\n            {busy ? \"Working…\" : \"Send tip\"}\n          </button>\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className=\"secondary\"\n            onClick={handleFaucet}\n            disabled={busy}\n            title=\"Local dev only: mint yourself 1,000 test USDC\"\n          >\n            Get test USDC\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </form>\n\n      {status && <p className=\"status\">{status}</p>}\n      {error && <p className=\"error\">{error}</p>}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction readableError(err: unknown): string {\n  const msg =\n    (err as { shortMessage?: string; message?: string })?.shortMessage ??\n    (err as { message?: string })?.message ??\n    \"Something went wrong.\";\n  if (/User rejected|denied/i.test(msg)) return \"Transaction rejected.\";\n  return msg;\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":553,"cache_read_input_tokens":42795,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":553},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"20b4b874-e367-47cf-b746-716131007b23","uuid":"e726182f-a635-4e7c-9c43-b99d42c4ba6f","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:37:27.205Z","request_id":"req_011CdMbxja5jGKbot7ehzKug"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMc1SGrhAacc5VEuqU2b","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01EmskP9L1TXugreRvmcnS2o","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T213208Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/frontend/components/TipFeed.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { formatUsdc, shortenAddress, timeAgo } from \"../lib/format\";\n\nexport type TipItem = {\n  from: `0x${string}`;\n  amount: bigint;\n  message: string;\n  timestamp: bigint;\n};\n\nexport function TipFeed({\n  tips,\n  isLoading,\n}: {\n  tips: readonly TipItem[] | undefined;\n  isLoading: boolean;\n}) {\n  // Show newest first.\n  const ordered = tips ? [...tips].reverse() : [];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card\">\n      <h2>Tip feed</h2>\n\n      {isLoading && !tips && <p className=\"muted\">Loading tips…</p>}\n\n      {tips && ordered.length === 0 && (\n        <p className=\"muted\">No tips yet. Be the first to drop one! 💙</p>\n      )}\n\n      <ul className=\"feed\">\n        {ordered.map((tip, i) => (\n          <li key={`${tip.timestamp}-${i}`} className=\"feed-item\">\n            <div className=\"feed-row\">\n              <span className=\"feed-amount\">{formatUsdc(tip.amount)} USDC</span>\n              <span className=\"muted feed-time\">{timeAgo(tip.timestamp)}</span>\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"feed-from muted\">\n              from {shortenAddress(tip.from)}\n            </div>\n            {tip.message && <div className=\"feed-message\">“{tip.message}”</div>}\n          </li>\n        ))}\n      </ul>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3430,"cache_read_input_tokens":43348,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3430},"output_tokens":7,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"20b4b874-e367-47cf-b746-716131007b23","uuid":"bfdd3d98-4750-46c6-b923-72bfeed20703","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:37:42.374Z","request_id":"req_011CdMc1R9tfapkfApZjniP2"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMc31FXALLELTbuWQrr3","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01B5A5zCE7Khv4o5yFQ8oaTa","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T213208Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/frontend/components/TipJarApp.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useCallback } from \"react\";\nimport { useReadContract } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { TIP_JAR_ADDRESS, tipJarAbi } from \"../contracts\";\nimport { Stats } from \"./Stats\";\nimport { TipFeed, type TipItem } from \"./TipFeed\";\nimport { TipForm } from \"./TipForm\";\n\nconst REFRESH_MS = 5000;\n\nexport function TipJarApp() {\n  const tips = useReadContract({\n    address: TIP_JAR_ADDRESS,\n    abi: tipJarAbi,\n    functionName: \"getTips\",\n    query: { refetchInterval: REFRESH_MS },\n  });\n\n  const totalTipped = useReadContract({\n    address: TIP_JAR_ADDRESS,\n    abi: tipJarAbi,\n    functionName: \"totalTipped\",\n    query: { refetchInterval: REFRESH_MS },\n  });\n\n  const tipCount = useReadContract({\n    address: TIP_JAR_ADDRESS,\n    abi: tipJarAbi,\n    functionName: \"tipCount\",\n    query: { refetchInterval: REFRESH_MS },\n  });\n\n  const jarBalance = useReadContract({\n    address: TIP_JAR_ADDRESS,\n    abi: tipJarAbi,\n    functionName: \"balance\",\n    query: { refetchInterval: REFRESH_MS },\n  });\n\n  const refreshAll = useCallback(() => {\n    tips.refetch();\n    totalTipped.refetch();\n    tipCount.refetch();\n    jarBalance.refetch();\n  }, [tips, totalTipped, tipCount, jarBalance]);\n\n  return (\n    <>\n      <Stats\n        totalTipped={totalTipped.data as bigint | undefined}\n        tipCount={tipCount.data as bigint | undefined}\n        jarBalance={jarBalance.data as bigint | undefined}\n      />\n\n      <div className=\"columns\">\n        <TipForm onTipped={refreshAll} />\n        <TipFeed\n          tips={tips.data as readonly TipItem[] | undefined}\n          isLoading={tips.isLoading}\n        />\n      </div>\n    </>\n  );\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":629,"cache_read_input_tokens":48036,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":629},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"20b4b874-e367-47cf-b746-716131007b23","uuid":"4265c110-deaf-4b70-92e6-44e271405ba1","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:38:00.310Z","request_id":"req_011CdMc2z9J89t8W6GYssSh5"}

\"./Stats\";\nimport { TipFeed, type TipItem } from \"./TipFeed\";\nimport { TipForm } from \"./TipForm\";\n\nconst REFRESH_MS = 5000;\n\nexport function TipJarApp() {\n  const tips = useReadContract({\n    address: TIP_JAR_ADDRESS,\n    abi: tipJarAbi,\n    functionName: \"getTips\",\n    query: { refetchInterval: REFRESH_MS },\n  });\n\n  const totalTipped = useReadContract({\n    address: TIP_JAR_ADDRESS,\n    abi: tipJarAbi,\n    functionName: \"totalTipped\",\n    query: { refetchInterval: REFRESH_MS },\n  });\n\n  const tipCount = useReadContract({\n    address: TIP_JAR_ADDRESS,\n    abi: tipJarAbi,\n    functionName: \"tipCount\",\n    query: { refetchInterval: REFRESH_MS },\n  });\n\n  const jarBalance = useReadContract({\n    address: TIP_JAR_ADDRESS,\n    abi: tipJarAbi,\n    functionName: \"balance\",\n    query: { refetchInterval: REFRESH_MS },\n  });\n\n  const refreshAll = useCallback(() => {\n    tips.refetch();\n    totalTipped.refetch();\n    tipCount.refetch();\n    jarBalance.refetch();\n  }, [tips, totalTipped, tipCount, jarBalance]);\n\n  return (\n    <>\n      <Stats\n        totalTipped={totalTipped.data as bigint | undefined}\n        tipCount={tipCount.data as bigint | undefined}\n        jarBalance={jarBalance.data as bigint | undefined}\n      />\n\n      <div className=\"columns\">\n        <TipForm onTipped={refreshAll} />\n        <TipFeed\n          tips={tips.data as readonly TipItem[] | undefined}\n          isLoading={tips.isLoading}\n        />\n      </div>\n    </>\n  );\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{\n  --bg: #f5f7fb;\n  --card: #ffffff;\n  --border: #e6e9f0;\n  --text: #1a1f36;\n  --muted: #6b7280;\n  --usdc: #2775ca;\n  --usdc-dark: #1f5fa8;\n  --error: #c0362c;\n  --ok: #1f8a4c;\n}\n\n* {\n  box-sizing: border-box;\n}\n\nhtml,\nbody {\n  margin: 0;\n  padding: 0;\n  background: var(--bg);\n  color: var(--text);\n  font-family:\n    -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, \"Segoe UI\", Roboto, Helvetica, Arial,\n    sans-serif;\n  line-height: 1.5;\n}\n\n.page {\n  max-width: 900px;\n  margin: 0 auto;\n  padding: 32px 20px 64px;\n}\n\n.header {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  gap: 16px;\n  margin-bottom: 28px;\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\n}\n\n.brand {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 14px;\n}\n\n.logo {\n  font-size: 40px;\n}\n\nh1 {\n  font-size: 24px;\n  margin: 0;\n}\n\nh2 {\n  font-size: 18px;\n  margin: 0 0 12px;\n}\n\n.subtitle {\n  margin: 2px 0 0;\n  font-size: 14px;\n}\n\n.muted {\n  color: var(--border);\n  border-radius: 14px;\n  padding: 16px;\n  text-align: center;\n}\n\n.stat-value {\n  font-size: 22px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  color: var(--usdc);\n}\n\n.stat-label {\n  font-size: 13px;\n  color: var(--muted);\n  margin-top: 2px;\n}\n\n.columns {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;\n  gap: 18px;\n  align-items: start;\n}\n\n.card {\n  background: var(--card);\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: 16px;\n  padding: 20px;\n}\n\n.card.warn {\n  border-color: #f0d58a;\n  background: #fff9e8;\n}\n\n.tip-form {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  gap: 14px;\n}\n\n.tip-form label {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  gap: 6px;\n  font-size: 14px;\n  font-weight: 600;\n}\n\n.tip-form input,\n.tip-form textarea {\n  font: inherit;\n  padding: 10px 12px;\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: 10px;\n  background: #fbfcfe;\n  resize: vertical;\n}\n\n.tip-form input:focus,\n.tip-form textarea:focus {\n  outline: 2px solid var(--usdc);\n  outline-offset: 0;\n  border-color: var(--usdc);\n}\n\n.counter {\n  align-self: flex-end;\n  font-size: 12px;\n  font-weight: 400;\n  color: var(--muted);\n}\n\n.actions {\n  display: flex;\n  gap: 10px;\n}\n\nbutton {\n  font: inherit;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  border-radius: 10px;\n  padding: 10px 16px;\n  border: 1px solid transparent;\n  font-weight: 600;\n}\n\nbutton:disabled {\n  opacity: 0.6;\n  cursor: not-allowed;\n}\n\nbutton.primary {\n  background: var(--usdc);\n  color: #fff;\n  flex: 1;\n}\n\nbutton.primary:hover:not(:disabled) {\n  background: var(--usdc-dark);\n}\n\nbutton.secondary {\n  background: #eef4fc;\n  color: var(--usdc-dark);\n  border-color: #d4e4f7;\n}\n\n.status {\n  color: var(--ok);\n  font-size: 14px;\n  margin: 12px 0 0;\n}\n\n.error {\n  color: var(--error);\n  font-size: 14px;\n  margin: 12px 0 0;\n  word-break: break-word;\n}\n\n.feed {\n  list-style: none;\n  margin: 0;\n  padding: 0;\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  gap: 12px;\n  max-height: 460px;\n  overflow-y: auto;\n}\n\n.feed-item {\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMcE3gfzHDU9iL9nXp5D","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01EPB5CDkjURaX2S5Z2u5AqA","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T213208Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/frontend/next.config.mjs","content":"/** @type {import('next').NextConfig} */\nconst nextConfig = {\n  reactStrictMode: true,\n  webpack: (config, { webpack }) => {\n    // RainbowKit -> wagmi's Base/Coinbase account connector transitively imports\n    // optional packages (pino-pretty, and unpublished @x402/* submodules used by\n    // Base Pay) that we don't use in this local app. Ignore them so the build\n    // doesn't fail trying to resolve modules that aren't installed.\n    config.plugins.push(\n      new webpack.IgnorePlugin({\n        resourceRegExp: /^(pino-pretty|lokijs|encoding)$|^@x402\\//,\n      }),\n    );\n    config.externals.push(\"pino-pretty\", \"lokijs\", \"encoding\");\n    return config;\n  },\n};\n\nexport default nextConfig;\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1187,"cache_read_input_tokens":58090,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1187},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"20b4b874-e367-47cf-b746-716131007b23","uuid":"b23cf787-05f1-4a5a-9a3f-3b4e37d16ecc","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:40:46.236Z","request_id":"req_011CdMcE2T1XxUBKwnzg4fte"}

Let me write it thoroughly and accurately."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":980,"cache_read_input_tokens":67396,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":980},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"20b4b874-e367-47cf-b746-716131007b23","uuid":"0326b09c-524a-4c88-9028-a31a523058e2","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:43:38.208Z","request_id":"req_011CdMcTLECcnzPhGXoB4o3P"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMcTMpSgZ6Ugr4S6rb1w","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01AtS4CehCfN3KV2MgWfXZgx","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T213208Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/README.md","content":"# USDC Tip Jar (Base)\n\nA minimal, self-contained **USDC tip jar** for Base:\n\n- An onchain **`TipJar`** contract that accepts USDC tips with a short message,\n  records a public tip feed, and lets the owner withdraw collected tips.\n- A **Next.js web app** with a connect-wallet flow, the live tip feed, and a\n  form to send a tip (handles the USDC `approve` + `tip` two-step for you).\n\nEverything runs **locally** against a local Anvil chain. On a local chain there\nis no real USDC, so the project deploys a `MockUSDC` (a 6-decimal ERC-20 with a\nfaucet) and points the tip jar at it. In production you'd instead point `TipJar`\nat the real **Base mainnet USDC**:\n`0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`.\n\n> This repo is intentionally **local-only** — nothing is deployed to a public\n> network.\n\n---\n\n## Project layout\n\n```\n.\n├── contracts/                # Foundry project (Solidity)\n│   ├── src/TipJar.sol         # the tip jar\n│   ├── src/MockUSDC.sol       # local-only test USDC (6 decimals + faucet)\n│   ├── script/Deploy.s.sol    # deploys both, seeds USDC, writes frontend config\n│   └── test/TipJar.t.sol      # unit + fuzz tests\n└── frontend/                  # Next.js app (wagmi + RainbowKit + viem)\n    ├── app/                   # pages, layout, providers\n    ├── components/            # TipForm, TipFeed, Stats, ...\n    ├── contracts/             # ABIs + deployment.json (generated by Deploy.s.sol)\n    └── lib/                    # wagmi config + formatting helpers\n```\n\n---\n\n## Prerequisites\n\n- **Foundry** (`forge`, `anvil`, `cast`) — install:\n  ```bash\n  curl -L https://foundry.paradigm.xyz | bash\n  foundryup\n  ```\n- **Node.js 20 or 22** (LTS) and **npm**. Node ≥ 23 can break the Next build, so\n  stick to an LTS release (e.g. `nvm use 22`).\n- A browser wallet such as **MetaMask** to connect and send tips.\n\n---\n\n## Setup\n\nYou'll use **three terminals**: one for the local chain, one to deploy, one for\nthe web app.\n\n### 1. Start a local chain\n\n```bash\nanvil\n```\n\nLeave this running. It prints 10 funded test accounts and their private keys.\n**Account #0** is the default deployer and tip-jar owner:\n\n- Address: `0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266`\n- Private key: `0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80`\n\n### 2. Deploy the contracts\n\nIn a second terminal:\n\n```bash\ncd contracts\nforge install                 # first time only: fetch forge-std + OpenZeppelin\nforge test                    # optional: run the contract tests\nforge script script/Deploy.s.sol:Deploy \\\n  --rpc-url http://127.0.0.1:8545 \\\n  --broadcast\n```\n\nThis deploys `MockUSDC` + `TipJar`, mints 10,000 test USDC to the deployer, and\nwrites the deployed addresses to `frontend/contracts/deployment.json` so the web\napp can find them. Because Anvil deploys deterministically, the addresses are the\nsame every run:\n\n- `MockUSDC`: `0x5FbDB2315678afecb367f032d93F642f64180aa3`\n- `TipJar`:  `0xe7f1725E7734CE288F8367e1Bb143E90bb3F0512`\n\n> To deploy from a different account, set `PRIVATE_KEY` before running the\n> script (e.g. `PRIVATE_KEY=0x... forge script ...`).\n\n### 3. Run the web app\n\nIn a third terminal:\n\n```bash\ncd frontend\ncp .env.local.example .env.local   # optional; defaults work out of the box\nnpm install\nnpm run dev\n```\n\nOpen the printed URL (usually **http://localhost:3000**).\n\n### 4. Connect a wallet and tip\n\n1. In MetaMask, add the local network:\n   - **Network name:** Anvil (local)\n   - **RPC URL:** `http://127.0.0.1:8545`\n   - **Chain ID:** `31337`\n   - **Currency symbol:** ETH\n2. Import a test account using one of Anvil's printed private keys (Account #0\n   already holds 10,000 test USDC and ETH for gas).\n3. Click **Connect Wallet** in the app.\n4. Need test USDC? Click **Get test USDC** to mint yourself 1,000 from the\n   faucet (any account can do this locally).\n5. Enter an amount and an optional message and click **Send tip**. The app will\n   ask you to approve USDC (first tip only) and then send the tip. Your tip\n   appears at the top of the feed within a few seconds.\n\n---\n\n## How it works\n\n- **Tipping is a two-step ERC-20 flow.** USDC must be `approve`d before the tip\n  jar can pull it, so the form sends an `approve` transaction when your current\n  allowance is too low, then calls `TipJar.tip(amount, message)`.\n- **The feed** is read directly from the contract via `TipJar.getTips()` and\n  refreshes every few seconds (and immediately after you send a tip).\n- **Amounts use 6 decimals** (USDC), so `5 USDC` = `5000000` base units. The UI\n  converts for you.\n- **Withdrawals:** collected tips stay in the contract until the owner calls\n  `withdraw()`, which sends the full balance to the owner. The historical feed\n  is preserved after withdrawal.\n\n### Contract API (`TipJar`)\n\n| Function | Description |\n| --- | --- |\n| `tip(uint256 amount, string message)` | Send a USDC tip (requires prior `approve`). |\n| `getTips()` | All tips, oldest first. |\n| `getRecentTips(uint256 count)` | Most recent `count` tips, newest first. |\n| `tipCount()` / `totalTipped()` / `balance()` | Feed length, all-time total, current jar balance. |\n| `withdraw()` | Owner-only: send the jar's USDC balance to the owner. |\n| `transferOwnership(address)` | Owner-only: hand off withdrawal rights. |\n\n---\n\n## Running the tests\n\n```bash\ncd contracts\nforge test -vvv\n```\n\nCovers tipping + feed recording, USDC transfer, message/amount validation,\nowner-only withdrawal, and a fuzz test over tip amounts.\n\n---\n\n## Notes & troubleshooting\n\n- **\"Contracts not deployed yet\" banner or an empty feed:** make sure Anvil is\n  running and you've run the deploy step (which writes\n  `frontend/contracts/deployment.json`). If you restart Anvil, redeploy.\n- **Wrong network:** the app targets chain ID `31337`. RainbowKit will show a\n  \"Wrong network\" prompt if your wallet is elsewhere — switch to Anvil (local).\n- **`MockUSDC` is for local development only.** Never deploy it to a real\n  network; for Base you'd use the canonical USDC address above.\n- **WalletConnect:** MetaMask/injected wallets work without any config. To\n  enable WalletConnect-based wallets, set `NEXT_PUBLIC_WC_PROJECT_ID` in\n  `frontend/.env.local` to a project id from https://cloud.walletconnect.com.\n```\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":980,"cache_read_input_tokens":67396,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":980},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"20b4b874-e367-47cf-b746-716131007b23","uuid":"7414986b-dd71-4143-9608-4c3f2429f10d","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:44:14.631Z","request_id":"req_011CdMcTLECcnzPhGXoB4o3P"}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01AtS4CehCfN3KV2MgWfXZgx","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T213208Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/README.md (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"20b4b874-e367-47cf-b746-716131007b23","uuid":"df147a04-471d-4432-9d6f-e6d347800923","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:44:14.640Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T213208Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/README.md","content":"# USDC Tip Jar (Base)\n\nA minimal, self-contained **USDC tip jar** for Base:\n\n- An onchain **`TipJar`** contract that accepts USDC tips with a short message,\n  records a public tip feed, and lets the owner withdraw collected tips.\n- A **Next.js web app** with a connect-wallet flow, the live tip feed, and a\n  form to send a tip (handles the USDC `approve` + `tip` two-step for you).\n\nEverything runs **locally** against a local Anvil chain. On a local chain there\nis no real USDC, so the project deploys a `MockUSDC` (a 6-decimal ERC-20 with a\nfaucet) and points the tip jar at it. In production you'd instead point `TipJar`\nat the real **Base mainnet USDC**:\n`0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`.\n\n> This repo is intentionally **local-only** — nothing is deployed to a public\n> network.\n\n---\n\n## Project layout\n\n```\n.\n├── contracts/                # Foundry project (Solidity)\n│   ├── src/TipJar.sol         # the tip jar\n│   ├── src/MockUSDC.sol       # local-only test USDC (6 decimals + faucet)\n│   ├── script/Deploy.s.sol    # deploys both, seeds USDC, writes frontend config\n│   └── test/TipJar.t.sol      # unit + fuzz tests\n└── frontend/                  # Next.js app (wagmi + RainbowKit + viem)\n    ├── app/                   # pages, layout, providers\n    ├── components/            # TipForm, TipFeed, Stats, ...\n    ├── contracts/             # ABIs + deployment.json (generated by Deploy.s.sol)\n    └── lib/                    # wagmi config + formatting helpers\n```\n\n---\n\n## Prerequisites\n\n- **Foundry** (`forge`, `anvil`, `cast`) — install:\n  ```bash\n  curl -L https://foundry.paradigm.xyz | bash\n  foundryup\n  ```\n- **Node.js 20 or 22** (LTS) and **npm**. Node ≥ 23 can break the Next build, so\n  stick to an LTS release (e.g. `nvm use 22`).\n- A browser wallet such as **MetaMask** to connect and send tips.\n\n---\n\n## Setup\n\nYou'll use **three terminals**: one for the local chain, one to deploy, one for\nthe web app.\n\n### 1. Start a local chain\n\n```bash\nanvil\n```\n\nLeave this running. It prints 10 funded test accounts and their private keys.\n**Account #0** is the default deployer and tip-jar owner:\n\n- Address: `0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266`\n- Private key: `0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80`\n\n### 2. Deploy the contracts\n\nIn a second terminal:\n\n```bash\ncd contracts\nforge install                 # first time only: fetch forge-std + OpenZeppelin\nforge test                    # optional: run the contract tests\nforge script script/Deploy.s.sol:Deploy \\\n  --rpc-url http://127.0.0.1:8545 \\\n  --broadcast\n```\n\nThis deploys `MockUSDC` + `TipJar`, mints 10,000 test USDC to the deployer, and\nwrites the deployed addresses to `frontend/contracts/deployment.json` so the web\napp can find them. Because Anvil deploys deterministically, the addresses are the\nsame every run:\n\n- `MockUSDC`: `0x5FbDB2315678afecb367f032d93F642f64180aa3`\n- `TipJar`:  `0xe7f1725E7734CE288F8367e1Bb143E90bb3F0512`\n\n> To deploy from a different account, set `PRIVATE_KEY` before running the\n> script (e.g. `PRIVATE_KEY=0x... forge script ...`).\n\n### 3. Run the web app\n\nIn a third terminal:\n\n```bash\ncd frontend\ncp .env.local.example .env.local   # optional; defaults work out of the box\nnpm install\nnpm run dev\n```\n\nOpen the printed URL (usually **http://localhost:3000**).\n\n### 4. Connect a wallet and tip\n\n1. In MetaMask, add the local network:\n   - **Network name:** Anvil (local)\n   - **RPC URL:** `http://127.0.0.1:8545`\n   - **Chain ID:** `31337`\n   - **Currency symbol:** ETH\n2. Import a test account using one of Anvil's printed private keys (Account #0\n   already holds 10,000 test USDC and ETH for gas).\n3. Click **Connect Wallet** in the app.\n4. Need test USDC? Click **Get test USDC** to mint yourself 1,000 from the\n   faucet (any account can do this locally).\n5. Enter an amount and an optional message and click **Send tip**. The app will\n   ask you to approve USDC (first tip only) and then send the tip. Your tip\n   appears at the top of the feed within a few seconds.\n\n---\n\n## How it works\n\n- **Tipping is a two-step ERC-20 flow.** USDC must be `approve`d before the tip\n  jar can pull it, so the form sends an `approve` transaction when your current\n  allowance is too low, then calls `TipJar.tip(amount, message)`.\n- **The feed** is read directly from the contract via `TipJar.getTips()` and\n  refreshes every few seconds (and immediately after you send a tip).\n- **Amounts use 6 decimals** (USDC), so `5 USDC` = `5000000` base units. The UI\n  converts for you.\n- **Withdrawals:** collected tips stay in the contract until the owner calls\n  `withdraw()`, which sends the full balance to the owner. The historical feed\n  is preserved after withdrawal.\n\n### Contract API (`TipJar`)\n\n| Function | Description |\n| --- | --- |\n| `tip(uint256 amount, string message)` | Send a USDC tip (requires prior `approve`). |\n| `getTips()` | All tips, oldest first. |\n| `getRecentTips(uint256 count)` | Most recent `count` tips, newest first. |\n| `tipCount()` / `totalTipped()` / `balance()` | Feed length, all-time total, current jar balance. |\n| `withdraw()` | Owner-only: send the jar's USDC balance to the owner. |\n| `transferOwnership(address)` | Owner-only: hand off withdrawal rights. |\n\n---\n\n## Running the tests\n\n```bash\ncd contracts\nforge test -vvv\n```\n\nCovers tipping + feed recording, USDC transfer, message/amount validation,\nowner-only withdrawal, and a fuzz test over tip amounts.\n\n---\n\n## Notes & troubleshooting\n\n- **\"Contracts not deployed yet\" banner or an empty feed:** make sure Anvil is\n  running and you've run the deploy step (which writes\n  `frontend/contracts/deployment.json`). If you restart Anvil, redeploy.\n- **Wrong network:** the app targets chain ID `31337`. RainbowKit will show a\n  \"Wrong network\" prompt if your wallet is elsewhere — switch to Anvil (local).\n- **`MockUSDC` is for local development only.** Never deploy it to a real\n  network; for Base you'd use the canonical USDC address above.\n- **WalletConnect:** MetaMask/injected wallets work without any config. To\n  enable WalletConnect-based wallets, set `NEXT_PUBLIC_WC_PROJECT_ID` in\n  `frontend/.env.local` to a project id from https://cloud.walletconnect.com.\n```\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01XSKxxzS2Zj8oaXbDJgBQ4s","type":"tool_result","content":"The file /Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T213208Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/README.md has been updated successfully. (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"20b4b874-e367-47cf-b746-716131007b23","uuid":"ddfc81b4-9991-4de6-a090-191401a3ee22","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:44:46.860Z","tool_use_result":{"filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-07-24T213208Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/README.md","oldString":"```bash\ncd contracts\nforge install                 # first time only: fetch forge-std + OpenZeppelin\nforge test                    # optional: run the contract tests\nforge script script/Deploy.s.sol:Deploy \\\n  --rpc-url http://127.0.0.1:8545 \\\n  --broadcast\n```","newString":"```bash\ncd contracts\n# Dependencies (forge-std, OpenZeppelin) are vendored under lib/. If lib/ is\n# missing, fetch them with:\n#   forge install foundry-rs/forge-std OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-contracts@v5.1.0\nforge test                    # optional: run the contract tests\nforge script script/Deploy.s.sol:Deploy \\\n  --rpc-url http://127.0.0.1:8545 \\\n  --broadcast\n```","originalFile":"# USDC Tip Jar (Base)\n\nA minimal, self-contained **USDC tip jar** for Base:\n\n- An onchain **`TipJar`** contract that accepts USDC tips with a short message,\n  records a public tip feed, and lets the owner withdraw collected tips.\n- A **Next.js web app** with a connect-wallet flow, the live tip feed, and a\n  form to send a tip (handles the USDC `approve` + `tip` two-step for you).\n\nEverything runs **locally** against a local Anvil chain. On a local chain there\nis no real USDC, so the project deploys a `MockUSDC` (a 6-decimal ERC-20 with a\nfaucet) and points the tip jar at it. In production you'd instead point `TipJar`\nat the real **Base mainnet USDC**:\n`0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`.\n\n> This repo is intentionally **local-only** — nothing is deployed to a public\n> network.\n\n---\n\n## Project layout\n\n```\n.\n├── contracts/                # Foundry project (Solidity)\n│   ├── src/TipJar.sol         # the tip jar\n│   ├── src/MockUSDC.sol       # local-only test USDC (6 decimals + faucet)\n│   ├── script/Deploy.s.sol    # deploys both, seeds USDC, writes frontend config\n│   └── test/TipJar.t.sol      # unit + fuzz tests\n└── frontend/                  # Next.js app (wagmi + RainbowKit + viem)\n    ├── app/                   # pages, layout, providers\n    ├── components/            # TipForm, TipFeed, Stats, ...\n    ├── contracts/             # ABIs + deployment.json (generated by Deploy.s.sol)\n    └── lib/                    # wagmi config + formatting helpers\n```\n\n---\n\n## Prerequisites\n\n- **Foundry** (`forge`, `anvil`, `cast`) — install:\n  ```bash\n  curl -L https://foundry.paradigm.xyz | bash\n  foundryup\n  ```\n- **Node.js 20 or 22** (LTS) and **npm**. Node ≥ 23 can break the Next build, so\n  stick to an LTS release (e.g. `nvm use 22`).\n- A browser wallet such as **MetaMask** to connect and send tips.\n\n---\n\n## Setup\n\nYou'll use **three terminals**: one for the local chain, one to deploy, one for\nthe web app.\n\n### 1. Start a local chain\n\n```bash\nanvil\n```\n\nLeave this running. It prints 10 funded test accounts and their private keys.\n**Account #0** is the default deployer and tip-jar owner:\n\n- Address: `0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266`\n- Private key: `0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80`\n\n### 2. Deploy the contracts\n\nIn a second terminal:\n\n```bash\ncd contracts\nforge install                 # first time only: fetch forge-std + OpenZeppelin\nforge test                    # optional: run the contract tests\nforge script script/Deploy.s.sol:Deploy \\\n  --rpc-url http://127.0.0.1:8545 \\\n  --broadcast\n```\n\nThis deploys `MockUSDC` + `TipJar`, mints 10,000 test USDC to the deployer, and\nwrites the deployed addresses to `frontend/contracts/deployment.json` so the web\napp can find them. Because Anvil deploys deterministically, the addresses are the\nsame every run:\n\n- `MockUSDC`: `0x5FbDB2315678afecb367f032d93F642f64180aa3`\n- `TipJar`:  `0xe7f1725E7734CE288F8367e1Bb143E90bb3F0512`\n\n> To deploy from a different account, set `PRIVATE_KEY` before running the\n> script (e.g. `PRIVATE_KEY=0x... forge script ...`).\n\n### 3. Run the web app\n\nIn a third terminal:\n\n```bash\ncd frontend\ncp .env.local.example .env.local   # optional; defaults work out of the box\nnpm install\nnpm run dev\n```\n\nOpen the printed URL (usually **http://localhost:3000**).\n\n### 4. Connect a wallet and tip\n\n1. In MetaMask, add the local network:\n   - **Network name:** Anvil (local)\n   - **RPC URL:** `http://127.0.0.1:8545`\n   - **Chain ID:** `31337`\n   - **Currency symbol:** ETH\n2. Import a test account using one of Anvil's printed private keys (Account #0\n   already holds 10,000 test USDC and ETH for gas).\n3. Click **Connect Wallet** in the app.\n4. Need test USDC? Click **Get test USDC** to mint yourself 1,000 from the\n   faucet (any account can do this locally).\n5. Enter an amount and an optional message and click **Send tip**. The app will\n   ask you to approve USDC (first tip only) and then send the tip. Your tip\n   appears at the top of the feed within a few seconds.\n\n---\n\n## How it works\n\n- **Tipping is a two-step ERC-20 flow.** USDC must be `approve`d before the tip\n  jar can pull it, so the form sends an `approve` transaction when your current\n  allowance is too low, then calls `TipJar.tip(amount, message)`.\n- **The feed** is read directly from the contract via `TipJar.getTips()` and\n  refreshes every few seconds (and immediately after you send a tip).\n- **Amounts use 6 decimals** (USDC), so `5 USDC` = `5000000` base units. The UI\n  converts for you.\n- **Withdrawals:** collected tips stay in the contract until the owner calls\n  `withdraw()`, which sends the full balance to the owner. The historical feed\n  is preserved after withdrawal.\n\n### Contract API (`TipJar`)\n\n| Function | Description |\n| --- | --- |\n| `tip(uint256 amount, string message)` | Send a USDC tip (requires prior `approve`). |\n| `getTips()` | All tips, oldest first. |\n| `getRecentTips(uint256 count)` | Most recent `count` tips, newest first. |\n| `tipCount()` / `totalTipped()` / `balance()` | Feed length, all-time total, current jar balance. |\n| `withdraw()` | Owner-only: send the jar's USDC balance to the owner. |\n| `transferOwnership(address)` | Owner-only: hand off withdrawal rights. |\n\n---\n\n## Running the tests\n\n```bash\ncd contracts\nforge test -vvv\n```\n\nCovers tipping + feed recording, USDC transfer, message/amount validation,\nowner-only withdrawal, and a fuzz test over tip amounts.\n\n---\n\n## Notes & troubleshooting\n\n- **\"Contracts not deployed yet\" banner or an empty feed:** make sure Anvil is\n  running and you've run the deploy step (which writes\n  `frontend/contracts/deployment.json`). If you restart Anvil, redeploy.\n- **Wrong network:** the app targets chain ID `31337`. RainbowKit will show a\n  \"Wrong network\" prompt if your wallet is elsewhere — switch to Anvil (local).\n- **`MockUSDC` is for local development only.** Never deploy it to a real\n  network; for Base you'd use the canonical USDC address above.\n- **WalletConnect:** MetaMask/injected wallets work without any config. To\n  enable WalletConnect-based wallets, set `NEXT_PUBLIC_WC_PROJECT_ID` in\n  `frontend/.env.local` to a project id from https://cloud.walletconnect.com.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":72,"oldLines":7,"newStart":72,"newLines":9,"lines":[" "," ```bash"," cd contracts","-forge install                 # first time only: fetch forge-std + OpenZeppelin","+# Dependencies (forge-std, OpenZeppelin) are vendored under lib/. If lib/ is","+# missing, fetch them with:","+#   forge install foundry-rs/forge-std OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-contracts@v5.1.0"," forge test                    # optional: run the contract tests"," forge script script/Deploy.s.sol:Deploy \\","   --rpc-url http://127.0.0.1:8545 \\"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FTQ881LKcqzQKNqN7CMLDo","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t---\n2\tname: rainbowkit-wagmi-next-x402-build-fix\n3\tdescription: RainbowKit+wagmi+Next.js dev build fails on @x402 / async-storage optional deps — fix with IgnorePlugin\n4\tmetadata: \n5\t  node_type: memory\n6\t  type: reference\n7\t  originSessionId: d5c57ae0-5ba4-42e5-9286-490e29d2bfcb\n8\t  modified: 2026-07-24T21:19:25.820Z\n9\t---\n10\t\n11\tRainbowKit v2 + wagmi v2 + Next.js (webpack) dev server throws `Module not found: Can't resolve '@x402/evm/upto/client'` (and `@x402/core/client`, `@x402/evm/exact/client`). Chain: `@rainbow-me/rainbowkit` → `wagmi/connectors` barrel → `@wagmi/connectors/baseAccount` → `@base-org/account` → `@coinbase/cdp-sdk` lazily `import()`s x402 payment modules that aren't installed. The barrel import means avoiding the Coinbase/Base connector (e.g. via `connectorsForWallets`) does NOT fix it.\n12\t\n13\tFix in `next.config.js` webpack (resolve.alias `false` did NOT work for these subpath dynamic imports; IgnorePlugin does):\n14\t\n15\t```js\n16\twebpack: (config, { webpack }) => {\n17\t  config.plugins.push(new webpack.IgnorePlugin({ resourceRegExp: /^@x402\\// }));\n18\t  // also silences a harmless @metamask/sdk RN warning:\n19\t  config.plugins.push(new webpack.IgnorePlugin({ resourceRegExp: /^@react-native-async-storage\\/async-storage$/ }));\n20\t  return config;\n21\t}\n22\t```\n23\t\n24\tAlso push `'pino-pretty','lokijs','encoding'` to `config.externals`. The ignored modules are never executed at runtime for injected/MetaMask flows.\n25\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"20b4b874-e367-47cf-b746-716131007b23","uuid":"fcdbec40-df83-44b2-9ae1-c26388594fa9","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:45:08.275Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/.claude/projects/-Users-spencerfaber-dev-ethskills-evals/memory/rainbowkit-wagmi-next-x402-build-fix.md","content":"---\nname: rainbowkit-wagmi-next-x402-build-fix\ndescription: RainbowKit+wagmi+Next.js dev build fails on @x402 / async-storage optional deps — fix with IgnorePlugin\nmetadata: \n  node_type: memory\n  type: reference\n  originSessionId: d5c57ae0-5ba4-42e5-9286-490e29d2bfcb\n  modified: 2026-07-24T21:19:25.820Z\n---\n\nRainbowKit v2 + wagmi v2 + Next.js (webpack) dev server throws `Module not found: Can't resolve '@x402/evm/upto/client'` (and `@x402/core/client`, `@x402/evm/exact/client`). Chain: `@rainbow-me/rainbowkit` → `wagmi/connectors` barrel → `@wagmi/connectors/baseAccount` → `@base-org/account` → `@coinbase/cdp-sdk` lazily `import()`s x402 payment modules that aren't installed. The barrel import means avoiding the Coinbase/Base connector (e.g. via `connectorsForWallets`) does NOT fix it.\n\nFix in `next.config.js` webpack (resolve.alias `false` did NOT work for these subpath dynamic imports; IgnorePlugin does):\n\n```js\nwebpack: (config, { webpack }) => {\n  config.plugins.push(new webpack.IgnorePlugin({ resourceRegExp: /^@x402\\// }));\n  // also silences a harmless @metamask/sdk RN warning:\n  config.plugins.push(new webpack.IgnorePlugin({ resourceRegExp: /^@react-native-async-storage\\/async-storage$/ }));\n  return config;\n}\n```\n\nAlso push `'pino-pretty','lokijs','encoding'` to `config.externals`. The ignored modules are never executed at runtime for injected/MetaMask flows.\n","numLines":25,"startLine":1,"totalLines":25}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMcc8hf65zgKan1z28mV","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. Here's what I built and verified.\n\n## USDC Tip Jar for Base — complete and running locally\n\nA self-contained project in `workspace/`, split into an onchain contract and a web app.\n\n### Contracts (`contracts/` — Foundry)\n- **`TipJar.sol`** — accepts USDC tips via `tip(amount, message)`, records a public feed (`getTips` / `getRecentTips`), tracks `totalTipped`/`balance`, and lets the owner `withdraw()`. Uses OpenZeppelin `SafeERC20`; validates amount and message length.\n- **`MockUSDC.sol`** — a 6-decimal ERC-20 with a `faucet()` for local testing (real Base USDC `0x8335…2913` has no balances on a local chain, so a mock stands in; the real address is documented for production).\n- **`Deploy.s.sol`** — deploys both, seeds the deployer with 10,000 test USDC, and writes addresses to `frontend/contracts/deployment.json`.\n- **10 tests pass** (`forge test`): feed recording, USDC transfer, approval/amount/message validation, owner-only withdrawal, and a fuzz test.\n\n### Frontend (`frontend/` — Next.js + wagmi + RainbowKit + viem)\n- Connect-wallet flow (RainbowKit), a **tip feed** that reads `getTips()` and auto-refreshes, a **send form** that handles the USDC `approve` → `tip` two-step, a stats row, and a faucet button.\n\n### What I verified\n- `forge build` + `forge test` → **10/10 pass**.\n- Deployed to a local Anvil, confirmed `TipJar.usdc()` points at the mock.\n- `next build` → **clean production build** (needed the `@x402/*` IgnorePlugin fix from my notes — it hit `next build` as a hard error this time).\n- `next dev` served the page (HTTP 200) against the live deployment.\n- Ran the real **approve → tip → read** flow via `cast`: `tipCount=1`, `totalTipped=5 USDC`, and `getTips()` returned the tip with its message — the exact path the UI drives.\n\n### README.md\nDocuments prerequisites (Foundry, Node 20/22), the three-terminal setup (anvil → deploy → `npm run dev`), MetaMask config for chain 31337, the faucet, the tipping flow, the contract API, and troubleshooting.\n\n**Nothing was deployed to any public network** — anvil and the dev server were shut down, leaving the working local project and README as the deliverable. To run it: `anvil`, then deploy, then `cd frontend && npm install && npm run dev`.\n\nOne note: I built and tested with **Node 22** (via nvm) rather than the default Node 25, since Node ≥23 has bitten Next builds before — the README calls this out."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":867,"cache_read_input_tokens":77796,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":867},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"20b4b874-e367-47cf-b746-716131007b23","uuid":"155003a3-40a6-4be4-9d5b-32283d561747","timestamp":"2026-07-24T21:45:32.225Z","request_id":"req_011CdMcc7Yx8gNR1Rx3Nn9hm"}

Here's what I built and verified.\n\n## USDC Tip Jar for Base — complete and running locally\n\nA self-contained project in `workspace/`, split into an onchain contract and a web app.\n\n### Contracts (`contracts/` — Foundry)\n- **`TipJar.sol`** — accepts USDC tips via `tip(amount, message)`, records a public feed (`getTips` / `getRecentTips`), tracks `totalTipped`/`balance`, and lets the owner `withdraw()`. Uses OpenZeppelin `SafeERC20`; validates amount and message length.\n- **`MockUSDC.sol`** — a 6-decimal ERC-20 with a `faucet()` for local testing (real Base USDC `0x8335…2913` has no balances on a local chain, so a mock stands in; the real address is documented for production).\n- **`Deploy.s.sol`** — deploys both, seeds the deployer with 10,000 test USDC, and writes addresses to `frontend/contracts/deployment.json`.\n- **10 tests pass** (`forge test`): feed recording, USDC transfer, approval/amount/message validation, owner-only withdrawal, and a fuzz test.\n\n### Frontend (`frontend/` — Next.js + wagmi + RainbowKit + viem)\n- Connect-wallet flow (RainbowKit), a **tip feed** that reads `getTips()` and auto-refreshes, a **send form** that handles the USDC `approve` → `tip` two-step, a stats row, and a faucet button.\n\n### What I verified\n- `forge build` + `forge test` → **10/10 pass**.\n- Deployed to a local Anvil, confirmed `TipJar.usdc()` points at the mock.\n- `next build` → **clean production build** (needed the `@x402/*` IgnorePlugin fix from my notes — it hit `next build` as a hard error this time).\n- `next dev` served the page (HTTP 200) against the live deployment.\n- Ran the real **approve → tip → read** flow via `cast`: `tipCount=1`, `totalTipped=5 USDC`, and `getTips()` returned the tip with its message — the exact path the UI drives.\n\n### README.md\nDocuments prerequisites (Foundry, Node 20/22), the three-terminal setup (anvil → deploy → `npm run dev`), MetaMask config for chain 31337, the faucet, the tipping flow, the contract API, and troubleshooting.\n\n**Nothing was deployed to any public network** — anvil and the dev server were shut down, leaving the working local project and README as the deliverable. To run it: `anvil`, then deploy, then `cd frontend && npm install && npm run dev`.\n\nOne note: I built and tested with **Node 22** (via nvm) rather than the default Node 25, since Node ≥23 has bitten Next builds before — the README calls this out.","ttft_ms":11228,"ttft_stream_ms":1417,"time_to_request_ms":39,"type":"result","duration_ms":803033,"uuid":"43cea082-53d8-4a26-9f96-1d7aa3131cab"}
